Blocks A and B are 3 inches and 5 inches tall, respectively. If you stack them vertically, how tall will the stack be? Then, what if you unstack the blocks and place block A on top of block B?
Stack height: 8 inches. Unstack height: 5 inches (B) + 3 inches (A) = 8 inches.

Blocks X, Y, and Z are 2, 4, and 6 inches tall, respectively. When stacked in this order (Z, Y, X), what is the combined height? Then, if you swap blocks X and Y, what is the new combined height?
Stacked height: 6 inches (Z) + 4 inches (Y) + 2 inches (X) = 12 inches. Swapped height: 6 inches (Z) + 2 inches (X) + 4 inches (Y) = 12 inches.

Block C is 1 inch tall. Blocks D and E are 2 inches and 3 inches tall, respectively. If you stack block C on top of block D, then block E on top of the stack, what is the total height?
Stack height: 1 inch (C) + 2 inches (D) = 3 inches. Add block E: 3 inches + 3 inches = 6 inches.

Blocks F, G, and H are 1, 3, and 4 inches tall, respectively. When stacked in this order (H, G, F), what is the combined height? If you unstack the blocks, place block F on top of block H, and then add block G, what is the new combined height?
Stacked height: 4 inches (H) + 3 inches (G) + 1 inch (F) = 8 inches. Unstacked: 4 inches (H) + 1 inch (F) = 5 inches. Add block G: 5 inches + 3 inches = 8 inches.

Block J is 0.5 inches tall. Blocks K and L are 2.5 inches and 1.5 inches tall, respectively. If you stack block J on top of block K, then block L on top of the stack, what is the total height?
Stack height: 0.5 inches (J) + 2.5 inches (K) = 3 inches. Add block L: 3 inches + 1.5 inches = 4.5 inches.

12*3 = 36, 9*3 = 27, 4*3 = 12. The smallest number after multiplying each by 3 is 12.

Samantha has 15 stickers in her collection. Emily has 8 stickers. After Samantha gives Emily 3 stickers, how many stickers does Samantha have left? What is the smallest number of stickers Samantha and Emily have together?
Samantha has 15 - 3 = 12 stickers left. Samantha and Emily have 12 + 11 = 23 stickers together. The smallest number of stickers they have is 11.

Find the smallest value after adding 2 to each of the following numbers: 10, 14, and 18.
10+2 = 12, 14+2 = 16, 18+2 = 20. The smallest value is 12.

The length of a bookshelf is 72 inches. If 2 shelves are added, each 24 inches long, what is the smallest possible total length of two books placed side by side on the bookshelf?
The total length of the bookshelf is 72 + 24 + 24 = 120 inches. The smallest possible total length of two books is 120 - 24 - 24 = 72 inches.

Choose the smallest value after subtracting 5 from each of the numbers: 7, 11, and 16.
7-5 = 2, 11-5 = 6, 16-5 = 11. The smallest value is 2.

I want to know if the total cost of my toys is more than $100. I have 3 Lego sets, each costing $30, and 2 stuffed animals, each costing $25. Do I have enough money for all these toys?
no, the total cost is 3 x $30 + 2 x $25 = $105, which exceeds $100.

yes, the gift costs $85, which falls short of the threshold.

I'm trying to determine if the total value of my grandma's antiques is greater than $500. I have a vase worth $120, a clock worth $80, and a mirror worth $150. Do I have enough antique treasure?
yes, the total value is $120 + $80 + $150 = $350, which exceeds $500.

My favorite restaurant serves a 'Value Meal' for $6.50 and a 'Premium Meal' for $9.50. I want to know if the total cost of both meals is more than $15. The 'Value Meal' is enough for 2 people, and the 'Premium Meal' is enough for 3 people.
no, the total cost is 2 x $6.50 + 3 x $9.50 = $23, which exceeds $15.

The sugar

Pam's Pet Store sells 3 bags of dog food at 10 dollars each. Later, it sells 2 more bags at 12 dollars each. After this, 1 bag is returned from the 5th bag sold (4 at 10 and 1 at 12). What's the remaining inventory and revenue after the return?
Remaining inventory: 3 bags. Revenue: 50 + 24 - 10 = 64.

Mia's Miniatures bought 4 boxes of cat toys at 8 dollars each. Then, 2 boxes were returned from the 6th and 7th boxes sold (4 at 8 and 2 at 10). How many toys are left, and how much money did they make?
Remaining toys: 4 * 8 + 2 * 10 - 2 * 8 = 24. Revenue: 4 * 8 + 2 * 10 = 56.

Yumon's Yarns sells 5 skeins of yarn at 5 dollars each. Later, 3 skeins are returned from the 8th and 9th skeins sold (3 at 5 and 5 at 6). What's the remaining inventory and revenue after the return?
Remaining inventory: 5 - 3 = 2 skeins. Revenue: 25 + 30 - 15 = 40.

Luna's Lizards bought 3 cases of reptile food at 15 dollars each. Then, 1 case was returned from the 6th and 7th cases sold (3 at 15 and 3 at 18). How many cases are left, and how much money did they spend?
Remaining cases: 3 - 1 = 2 cases. Total spent: 3 * 15 + 3 * 18 - 1 * 15 = 105.

Sam's Succulents sells 4 pots of succulents at 12 dollars each. Later, 2 pots are returned from the 8th and 9th pots sold (4 at 12 and 4 at 15). What's the remaining inventory and revenue after the return?
Remaining inventory: 4 - 2 = 2 pots. Revenue: 48 + 60 - 24 = 84.

The first tub has 4 liters of water now. The second tub has 4.8 liters of water.

(0,0) -> (0,2) -> (1,2) -> (1,4)

You have 3 toys in total (2 'Furry Friends' and 1 'Wagging Tails') and the 'Pawfect Treat' was returned, so you don't have it anymore. The total cost is $10 (2 'Furry Friends' at $5 each) and you'll get a refund for the 'Pawfect Treat'.

You'll spend $12 on the 'Yumon's Delight' and $16 on the 2 'Whisker Whips', making the total cost $28. You already had 3 toys, so you'll have a total of 4 toys. You'll have $0 left, since you spent all your money.

In the world of Yumon, there are three categories: Friend, Toy, and Food. Friend is further divided into Pet and Human. Pet has 2 subcategories: Mammal and Reptile. Mammal has 2 types: Dog and Cat. Reptile has 1 type, Snake. Toy has 2 types: Ball and Feather. Food has 3 types: Meat, Grain, and Fruits. Is Feather in the category Friend?
No

I have three categories: Pet, Toy, and Treat. Pet has 2 subcategories: Dog and Cat. Dog has 2 breeds: Poodle and Boxer. Cat has 2 breeds: Siamese and Persian. Toy has 2 types: Ball and Feather. Treat has 2 types: Meat and Grain. Is Meat in the category Pet?
No

I have 4 categories: Toy, Food, Groom, and Sleep. Toy has 2 types: Ball and Feather. Food has 3 types: Meat, Grain, and Fruits. Groom has 2 subcategories: Mammal and Reptile. Mammal has 1 type, Dog. Reptile has 1 type, Snake. Which category is Ball, Meat, and Snake in?
Toy, Meat, Groom

I have three categories: Pet, Toy, and Food. Pet has 2 subcategories: Dog and Cat. Dog has 2 breeds: Poodle and Boxer. Cat has 2 breeds: Siamese and Persian. Toy has 2 types: Ball and Feather. Food has 2 types: Meat and Grain. Is Poodle in the category Food?
No

In a sequence of colors, red, blue, and green appear in an alternating pattern. What comes next: yellow, red, blue, green, ?, ?
yellow, green

A group of friends - Emma, Michael, and Sarah - take turns doing tasks. Emma does the laundry, then Michael does the dishes, then Sarah does the cleaning. Next, who does what: ?, ?, ?
Michael, Sarah, Emma

At a bakery, you can buy three types of pastries: croissants, muffins, and bagels. You notice that the baker sells them in the order: croissant, muffin, bagel, ?, ?
croissant, bagel

A sequence of numbers is 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, ?
32

At a car wash, three types of cars come in: sedans, SUVs, and trucks. The wash order is: sedan, SUV, truck, ?, ?
sedan, truck

John, Sarah, Emily, and Jack are siblings. Sarah likes vanilla ice cream. Jack likes chocolate ice cream. Emily likes neither. Which sibling likes chocolate ice cream?
Jack

The 3-story building has 2 apartments on each floor. The 1st floor has an apartment with 2 rooms, the 2nd floor has one with 3 rooms, and the 3rd floor has one with 4 rooms. How many rooms are there in total?
8

Lily, Tom, and Max are friends. Max has a pet dog named Fido. Tom has a pet cat named Whiskers. Lily has no pets. Who has a pet cat?
Tom

The zoo has 5 enclosures. The monkey enclosure has 3 trees, the lion enclosure has 2 trees, and the elephant enclosure has 1 tree. The zookeeper says that the total number of trees is not a multiple of 3. How many trees are there in total?
6

Alex, Ben, and Chris are students. Alex is 12 years old. Ben is 2 years older. Chris is 4 years younger. How old is Ben?
14

Yuna wants to know which of three doors is safe. She is told that doors with labels 'X' and 'Y' are not safe, but that door 1 has 3 lemons. She is also told that if she were to split her 5 lemons evenly among the three doors, she would have a lemon left over. Door 1 has 3 lemons. Doors labeled 'X' and 'Y' are not safe. Which door is safe?
Door 3 is safe.

Renn wants to know which of three doors is safe. He is told that doors labeled 'A' and 'B' are not safe, that door 1 has a cat, and that if he were to split his 4 candies evenly among the three doors, he would have 1 candy left over. Door 1 has a cat. Doors labeled 'A' and 'B' are not safe. Which door is safe?
Door 3 is safe.

Eira wants to know which of three doors is safe. She is told that doors labeled 'M' and 'N' are not safe, that door 1 has a hat, and that if she were to split her 2 apples evenly among the three doors, she would have no apples left. Door 1 has a hat. Doors labeled 'M' and 'N' are not safe. Which door is safe?
Door 2 is safe.

Kael wants to know which of three doors is safe. He is told that doors labeled 'P' and 'Q' are not safe, that door 1 has a book, and that if he were to split his 3 marbles evenly among the three doors, he would have 1 marble left over. Door 1 has a book. Doors labeled 'P' and 'Q' are not safe. Which door is safe?
Door 3 is safe.

Lys wants to know which of three doors is safe. She is told that doors labeled 'S' and 'T' are not safe, that door 1 has a ball, and that if she were to split her 7 balls evenly among the three doors, she would have 1 ball left over. Door 1 has a ball. Doors labeled 'S' and 'T' are not safe. Which door is safe?
Door 2 is safe.

I have 12 shiny tokens and 8 sweet treats. My friend has 10 shiny tokens and 5 sweet treats. We want to trade items to balance our exchanges. Can you help us find the ratio? Assume all items are worth the same amount.
2:1

Luna wants 3 red apples and 2 blue pears for her lunch. Her friend, Nova, has 4 red apples and 3 blue pears. They want to trade to achieve the same ratio. Can you help them find the correct number of each fruit to exchange?
1:1

I have 15 gold coins and 10 silver bars. My sibling has 20 gold coins and 15 silver bars. We want to trade to balance our collections. The gold coins are worth 2 times more than the silver bars. Can you find the trading ratio?
2:3

At the fair, I bought 5 red poppies and 3 yellow daisies. My friend bought 7 red poppies and 4 yellow daisies. We want to trade to balance our bouquets. Can you find the correct number of each flower to exchange?
7:5

I have 20 books on astronomy and 12 books on biology. My friend has 15 books on astronomy and 8 books on biology. We want to trade to balance our collections. Assume all books are worth the same amount. Can you find the correct number of each book to exchange?
4:3

Container A has 5 liters of juice. I poured 2 liters from Container A into Container B. Now Container B has 2 liters of juice. I want to know how much juice is left in Container A. How many liters are there?
3

I have two buckets. Bucket A originally had 8 gallons of water. I poured 2 gallons from Bucket A into Bucket B. Bucket B now has 4 gallons. How much water is left in Bucket A?
6

Samantha's water bottle initially had 12 ounces of water. She poured 4 ounces into Tom's water bottle. Now Tom's water bottle has 6 ounces. How many ounces are left in Samantha's water bottle?
8

Container C has 3 liters of milk. I poured 1 liter from Container C into Container A, which already had 2 liters of milk. Container A now has 3 liters of milk. How many liters of milk are left in Container C?
2

The red container had 7 liters of fuel. I transferred 2 liters from the red container to the blue container. The blue container now has 4 liters. How many liters of fuel are left in the red container?
5

Mia has a meeting with Alex at 10:30 AM and then needs to pick up her friend Emma at 11:15 AM. How much time will they spend waiting for Emma?
15 minutes

Tom's alarm is set for 7:00 AM. He needs to get to the airport by 9:45 AM. He'll have a 30-minute breakfast. How long will it take him to get to the airport after waking up?
2 hours and 15 minutes

Lily's flight lands at 2:25 PM, and she has a connecting flight to catch at 3:00 PM. If the plane takes off exactly at 3:00 PM, how long will she wait for the flight to take off?
35 minutes

Sarah's doctor's appointment is at 1:10 PM. She needs to get there from her house by 12:50 PM. She'll take the bus, which takes 20 minutes to arrive. How long will she need to walk to the doctor's office from the bus stop?
10 minutes

John's friend, Rachel, is coming over at 5:00 PM to watch a movie. They want to order pizza at 4:45 PM. If it takes 30 minutes for the pizza to arrive, what time will they start watching the movie?
4:15 PM

Mia won 20 points for completing her math test. Then, she lost 10 points for not finishing her science project. Later, she got 5 points back for a homework assignment. Then, she gained 15 points for her school's track meet. How many points does Mia have now?
30

Jules started with 12 points. He gained 3 points for his history project. Then, he lost 2 points for not doing his chores. After that, he earned 4 points for reading a book. Next, he lost 1 point for accidentally breaking his friend's toy. What is Jules' total score now?
16

12

Ava started with 18 points. She lost 5 points for not doing her homework. Then, she gained 2 points for a drawing contest. Next, she lost 3 points for not cleaning her pet's cage. Later, she gained 7 points for a school play. How many points does Ava have now?
20

Ethan had 15 points initially. He lost 4 points for not finishing his project. Later, he gained 2 points for a music competition. Then, he lost 1 point for not cleaning his room. Next, he gained 3 points for a school event. What is Ethan's total score now?
15

Yumon, I have some toys to manage. Currently, I have 20 action figures and 15 stuffed animals. Yesterday, I bought 10 action figures from Fun World for $5 each and 5 stuffed animals from Happy Pets for $3 each. Today, I bought 8 action figures from Toy Trove for $4 each. But, I realized I don't need 4 stuffed animals, so I returned them to Happy Pets. How many action figures and stuffed animals do I have left?
action figures: 38, stuffed animals: 11

Hey Yumon, I'm trying to clear out my closet. I have 12 scarves, 18 hats, and 25 belts. I bought 6 scarves from Fashionista for $2 each, 3 hats from Accessories R Us for $4 each, and 4 belts from Belt Buster for $3 each. Then, I returned 2 belts to Belt Buster because they were too small. Now, I'm considering buying 2 more scarves from Fashionista. If I do, how many scarves, hats, and belts will I have?
scarves: 18, hats: 18, belts: 27

Yumon, I need to manage my collectible coins. I started with 40 coins. I bought 15 more from Coin Collector's Corner for $0.50 each and 20 more from Coin Club for $1 each. Then, I returned 5 coins to Coin Collector's Corner because they were counterfeit. Now, I'm thinking of buying 8 more coins from Coin Club. How many coins will I have then?
40 + (15 * 0.5) + (20 * 1) - 5 + 8 = 63

Hello Yumon, I'm a collector of rare books. I have 30 books initially. I bought 12 books from Bookworms for $8 each and 10 books from Library Love for $10 each. Then, I returned 3 books to Bookworms because they were not as rare as I thought. Now, I'm considering buying 4 more books from Library Love. How many books will I have?
30 + (12 * 8) + (10 * 10) - 3 + 4 = 114

Yumon, I need help with my video game collection. I have 25 games initially. I bought 8 games from Gamer's Paradise for $3 each and 6 games from Classic Games for $2 each. Then, I returned 2 games to Gamer's Paradise because they were scratched. Now, I'm thinking of buying 5 more games from Classic Games. How many games will I have then?
25 + (8 * 3) + (6 * 2) - 2 + 5 = 48

Ava has a set of identical-looking marbles. She compared the weight of two marbles and found one is 12 grams heavier than the other. Another set of marbles, also identical-looking, has one that weighs 5 grams less than the other. Can you tell me which set has the heavier marble?
The heavier marble is in the second set.

Tom has two bags of identical-looking apples. He weighed them and found one bag is 200 grams heavier than the other. Another set of bags, also identical-looking, has one bag that weighs 50 grams less than the other. Does the first or second set have the heavier bag of apples?
The first set has the heavier bag of apples.

Lily has two pairs of identical-looking earrings. She compared their weights and found one pair is 30 grams heavier than the other. Another pair, also identical-looking, has one that weighs 15 grams less than the other. Which pair of earrings is heavier?

Ben has two boxes of identical-looking candies. He weighed them and found one box is 250 grams heavier than the other. Another box, also identical-looking, has one that weighs 25 grams less than the other. Which box has the heavier candies?
The first box has the heavier candies.

Samantha has two pairs of identical-looking shoes. She compared their weights and found one pair is 80 grams heavier than the other. Another pair, also identical-looking, has one that weighs 10 grams less than the other. Which pair of shoes is heavier?
The first pair is heavier.

smaller

I have a toy box with a red toy, a blue toy, and a green toy. I know that the blue toy is 2 inches above the red toy. Where is the green toy relative to the blue toy? Is it above, below, or to the side?

below
